That\nundercuts the self-contained deployment story the Makefile already\nclaims for release-agent-cli.\n\nDecision (operator): vendor mbedTLS statically, following the SQLite\npattern (pinned version, checksum-verified fetch script, static archive\ntarget, no system-library fallback). git commit -m "docs(session-handoff): 📝 define exclusive session ownership and handoff\n\nSessions currently assume a single writer: nothing stops two TUIs from\nrestoring the same conversation and interleaving ledger writes, and\nnothing lets the agent run detached from a terminal. Both gaps are the\nsame gap - sessions need an owner of record, a polite way to transfer\nit, and a way for bystanders to watch.\n\nThis feature defines (FEATURE only; spec pending):\n- One live holder per session: a lease bound to (hostname, pid,\n  process_start_time) plus a heartbeat, so PID reuse cannot impersonate\n  a dead holder and stale leases are recoverable.\n- Explicit, consensual handoff: request -> holder modal (accept/deny/\n  timeout) -> drain at a turn boundary (Interrupt v1 semantics) ->\n  release -> requester adopts via the existing resume path. The\n  outgoing TUI blanks with a handoff message.\n- Headless holders: --headless detached processes that register, bind,\n  and report to SQLite with no TTY, under a deny-by-default unattended\n  approval policy (never silently permissive).\n- Observe mode: a bounded, read-only per-session event feed for\n  tailing a live run.\n\nStore-as-broker: every protocol step is a row in the shared SQLite\nstore; no sockets, no IPC daemon. Introduces the driver abstraction\n(one driver, many observers) that Remote Agent Link will adopt, which\nis why it is sequenced before the link." && git log --oneline -3 && echo '---' && git status --short

finally\n    S.Free;\n  end;\nend;\n\nprocedure TStoreTest.TestChildSessionLineage;\nvar\n  S: TSqliteStore;\n  Err: TStoreError;\n  Ws, Depth: Integer;\n  Root, Child, Parent, Lbl: string;\nbegin\n  S := OpenStore;\n  try\n    S.EnsureWorkspace('/tmp', Ws, Err);\n    AssertTrue('root', S.CreateSession(Ws, Root, Err));\n    AssertTrue('child', S.CreateChildSession(Ws, Root, 1, 'research', Child,\n      Err));\n    AssertTrue('child id differs', Root <> Child);\n    AssertTrue('lineage', S.SessionLineage(Child, Parent, Depth, Lbl, Err));\n    AssertEquals('parent', Root, Parent);\n    AssertEquals('depth', 1, Depth);\n    AssertEquals('label', 'research', Lbl);\n  finally\n    S.Free;\n  end;\nend;\n\nprocedure TStoreTest.TestChildrenOf;\nvar\n  S: TSqliteStore;\n  Err: TStoreError;\n  Ws: Integer;\n  Root, A, B: string;\n  Kids: TSessionSummaryArray;\nbegin\n  S := OpenStore;\n  try\n    S.EnsureWorkspace('/tmp', Ws, Err);\n    AssertTrue('root', S.CreateSession(Ws, Root, Err));\n    AssertTrue('a', S.CreateChildSession(Ws, Root, 1, 'a', A, Err));\n    AssertTrue('b', S.CreateChildSession(Ws, Root, 1, 'b', B, Err));\n    AssertTrue('children', S.ChildrenOf(Root, Kids, Err));\n    AssertEquals('two children', 2, Length(Kids));\n    { A root has no children of its own. }\n    AssertTrue('grandchildren none', S.ChildrenOf(A, Kids, Err));\n    AssertEquals('leaf has none', 0, Length(Kids));\n  finally\n    S.Free;\n  end;\nend;\n\nprocedure TStoreTest.TestSessionLineageUnknownFails;\nvar\n  S: TSqliteStore;\n  Err: TStoreError;\n  Depth: Integer;\n  Parent, Lbl: string;\nbegin\n  S := OpenStore;\n  try\n    AssertFalse('unknown', S.SessionLineage('nope', Parent, Depth, Lbl, Err));\n    AssertEquals('not found', Ord(seNotFound), Ord(Err.Code));\n  finally\n    S.Free;\n  end;\nend;\n\nprocedure TStoreTest.TestLineageMigrationV4IsAdditive;\nvar\n  S: TSqliteStore;\n  Err: TStoreError;\n  Cfg: TStoreConfig;\n  Migs: TMigrationArray;\n  Ws, Depth: Integer;\n  Sid, Parent, Lbl: string;\n  Msgs: TMessageRecordArray;\nbegin\n  Cfg.DatabasePath := FDbPath;\n  Cfg.BusyTimeoutMs := 5000;\n\n  { A v2-only database, as an install predating lineage would have. }\n  Migs := DefaultMigrations;\n  SetLength(Migs, 2);\n  S := TSqliteStore.Create(Cfg, Migs);\n  try\n    AssertTrue('open v2', S.Open(Err));\n    S.EnsureWorkspace('/tmp', Ws, Err);\n    S.CreateSession(Ws, Sid, Err);\n  finally\n    S.Free;\n  end;\n\n  { Reopen with the full migrations: v3 (mcp servers) and v4 (lineage) apply\n    and the v2 session survives. }\n  S := TSqliteStore.Create(Cfg);\n  try\n    AssertTrue('reopen', S.Open(Err));\n    AssertEquals('schema at v4', 4, RawReadUserVersion(FDbPath));\n    AssertTrue('lineage after migration',\n      S.SessionLineage(Sid, Parent, Depth, Lbl, Err));\n    AssertEquals('migrated root parent empty', '', Parent);\n    AssertTrue('history survives', S.QueryMessages(Sid, Msgs, Err));\n  finally\n    S.Free;\n  end;\nend;\n\nprocedure TStoreTest.TestMessageRefSessionRoundTrip;\nvar\n  S: TSqliteStore;\n  Err: TStoreError;\n  Ws: Integer;\n  Sid: string;\n  Msg: TMessageRecord;\n  Msgs: TMessageRecordArray;\nbegin\n  S := OpenStore;\n  try\n    S.EnsureWorkspace('/tmp', Ws, Err);\n    S.CreateSession(Ws, Sid, Err);\n    Msg := Default(TMessageRecord);\n    Msg.SessionId := Sid;\n    Msg.Role := mrAmbient;\n    Msg.Content := 'subagent finished';\n    Msg.RefSessionId := 'child-123';\n    Msg.DurationMs := 4200;\n    AssertTrue('append', S.AppendMessage(Msg, Err));\n    AssertTrue('query', S.QueryMessages(Sid, Msgs, Err));\n    AssertEquals('one', 1, Length(Msgs));\n    AssertEquals('role ambient', Ord(mrAmbient), Ord(Msgs[0].Role));\n    AssertEquals('ref session', 'child-123', Msgs[0].RefSessionId);\n    AssertEquals('elapsed', 4200, Msgs[0].DurationMs);\n  finally\n    S.Free;\n  end;\nend;\n\n{ A v2-only database (before mcp_servers) upgrades to v3 additively. }\nprocedure TStoreTest.TestMcpServerMigrationIsAdditive;\nvar\n  S: TSqliteStore;\n  Err: TStoreError;\n  Cfg: TStoreConfig;\n  Migs: TMigrationArray;\n  Ws, Found: Integer;\n  Rows: TMcpServerRowArray;\nbegin\n  Cfg.DatabasePath := FDbPath;\n  Cfg.BusyTimeoutMs := 5000;\n  Migs := DefaultMigrations;\n  SetLength(Migs, 2);   // v1 + v2 only\n  S := TSqliteStore.Create(Cfg, Migs);\n  try\n    AssertTrue('open v2', S.Open(Err));\n    S.EnsureWorkspace('/tmp', Ws, Err);\n  finally\n    S.Free;\n  end;\n\n  S := TSqliteStore.Create(Cfg);\n  try\n    AssertTrue('reopen at v3', S.Open(Err));\n    AssertTrue('find workspace survives', S.FindWorkspace('/tmp', Found, Err));\n    AssertEquals('same workspace', Ws, Found);\n    AssertTrue('mcp table usable', S.ListMcpServers(Rows, Err));\n    AssertEquals('empty', 0, Length(Rows));\n  finally\n    S.Free;\n  end;\nend;\n\nprocedure TStoreTest.TestSaveMcpServerUpserts;\nvar\n  S: TSqliteStore;\n  Err: 'npx';\n    R.Enabled := True;\n    AssertTrue('insert', S.SaveMcpServer(R, Err));\n    R.Command := 'mcp-server-github';\n    AssertTrue('upsert', S.SaveMcpServer(R, Err));\n    AssertTrue('list', S.ListMcpServers(Rows, Err));\n    AssertEquals('one row (upserted, not duplicated)', 1, Length(Rows));\n    AssertEquals('command updated', 'mcp-server-github', Rows[0].Command);\n  finally\n    S.Free;\n  end;\nend;\n\nprocedure TStoreTest.TestLoadMcpServersScoped;\nvar\n  S: TSqliteStore;\n  Err: TStoreError;\n  Ws: Integer;\n  G, W, Other: TMcpServerRow;\n  Rows: TMcpServerRowArray;\n  Ctx: TMcpConfigContext;\nbegin\n  S := OpenStore;\n  try\n    S.EnsureWorkspace('/tmp/ws', Ws, Err);\n    G := Default(TMcpServerRow);\n    G.Scope := msGlobal;\n    G.Name := 'global-srv';\n    G.Command := 'g';\n    W := Default(TMcpServerRow);\n    W.Scope := msWorkspace;\n    W.WorkspaceId := Ws;\n    W.Name := 'ws-srv';\n    W.Command := 'w';\n    Other := Default(TMcpServerRow);\n    Other.Scope := msWorkspace;\n    Other.WorkspaceId := Ws + 1000;\n    Other.Name := 'other-srv';\n    Other.Command := 'o';\n    AssertTrue('save global', S.SaveMcpServer(G, Err));\n    AssertTrue('save ws', S.SaveMcpServer(W, Err));\n    AssertTrue('save other', S.SaveMcpServer(Other, Err));\n\n    Ctx.WorkspaceId := Ws;\n    Ctx.AgentId := '';\n    AssertTrue('load', S.LoadMcpServers(Ctx, Rows, Err));\n    AssertEquals('global + this workspace only', 2, Length(Rows));\n  finally\n    S.Free;\n  end;\nend;\n\nprocedure TStoreTest.TestRemoveMcpServer;\nvar\n  S: 'temp';\n    R.Command := 'x';\n    AssertTrue('save', S.SaveMcpServer(R, Err));\n    AssertTrue('remove', S.RemoveMcpServer(R, Err));\n    AssertTrue('list', S.ListMcpServers(Rows, Err));\n The one shape that compiles is a generic METHOD on a single non-generic class (TStreamOps) that constructs concrete observer + observable classes. So each operator is a TStreamOps method over a small concrete class pair, and call sites compose 1:1 with the pipeline: TStreamOps.Map<A,B>(src, f), TStreamOps.Filter<A>(src, p).\n\nImplemented: Just, FromArray, Range, Empty, ThrowError, Defer, Map, Scan, Filter, Take, Skip, TakeWhile, SkipWhile, DistinctUntilChanged, First, ElementAt. Map gives composability (a Map -> Filter pipeline is tested). Deferred (documented): Buffer/Window/GroupBy/Pairwise/flattening (generic-inside-generic is forbidden), Count (concrete return ICEs), and the time-based operators.\n\nAlso fixes a subscriber/observer reference cycle that leaked on terminal; the concrete observables now wrap the observer in a TSubscriber, which clears its references on terminal.\n\nmake check green (1114 tests, leak-clean)." && git log --oneline -1

git add docs/features/Async_Core/FPC_Generics_Notes.md examples/async_bench.pas src/async/async.context.pas tests/test_async_concurrency.pas && git commit -m "fix(async): 🐛 the context is thread-local, not a process global (executor hang)" -m "The continuation-scoped context kept its state in module-level globals. That is wrong once concurrency enters: TExecutorScheduler runs jobs on many worker threads, each installing/restoring the job's captured context around Work(), so several workers concurrently SetLength the same managed save stack - heap corruption, a worker dies mid-job, FPending never returns to zero, and ExecutorScheduler.Drain spins forever. The symptom was a hang, not a crash.\n\nThe context state (installed snapshot, save stack, depth) is now threadvar, so each thread owns its context and the managed threadvar is released when the thread exits. ContextCurrent lazily creates an empty snapshot, because the initialization block runs only on the main thread and a fresh worker starts with nil.\n\nAlso extends async_bench (observable/operators/executor, and a bounded virtual-clock case with a NOTE that the timer queue is O(T^2)) and documents the thread-locals-vs-process-globals rule (plus cthreads, and a lock for a shared counter) in FPC_Generics_Notes.md.\n\nRegression test: 5000 jobs across 4 workers. make check green (1163 tests, leak-clean)." && git log --oneline -1
